# Production audit ledger

Created (UTC): 2026-10-29T01:27:48.206209+00:00
Append-only after creation. Current production revision at creation: `87f2ebbf35fe91f2a00772826f9f14a023f71582` (PR #148), read in isolated Chrome from `/api/version` and equal to `origin/main`.

## Status vocabulary

- **HISTORICAL-FIX-RECORD:** a prior transcript/report claims a fix; not current-run proof.
- **UNVERIFIED:** not yet driven by this audit in the real browser.
- **BLOCKED:** requires unavailable credentials, owner approval, a destructive action, a paid job, or a state that cannot safely be created.
- **PASS/FAIL:** only after fresh evidence in `e2e-evidence/run-20261029T010233Z-full-functional/` has been personally inspected.

## Phase 0 — corpus inventory and reconciliation

- Full Claude Code corpus scanned for inventory: **931 JSONL files** (237 main + 694 subagent), **1,184,294 records**, **0 invalid JSON lines**. Machine inventory: `.local/session-mining/corpus-inventory.json`.
- Earlier consolidated findings: **369 unique IDs from 58 fully read findings files**, reproduced in Appendix A with their original fixing-PR/open and verification states.
- Latest cycle: **760 unique UI interactions** split as 66 public-core, 63 public-secondary, 63 accessibility, 81 member, 239 admin-content, 116 admin-AI and 132 admin-operations. The older 229/531 summary is not used as the denominator.
- API reconciliation: the archived and current OpenAPI documents both contain **192 operations**, identical method/path pairs. The prior prose claim of 196 is retained as a historical claim, not used as the current denominator. Current machine inventory: `e2e-evidence/run-20261029T010233Z-full-functional/step-02-openapi-inventory.json`.
- Cycle-7 candidate triage failed at 2026-10-28T17:43Z after quota errors. Eight later candidates therefore remain untriaged here rather than confirmed or discarded.
- Fresh source worktree for inspection/remediation: `/tmp/aw-full-audit-20261028` on branch `audit/prod-20261028` at PR #148. The dirty, 114-commit-behind main checkout is preserved untouched.

## Process blockers and open GitHub items

| Item | Current disposition |
|---|---|
| #76 dedicated audit admin | **BLOCKED:** `AUDIT_ADMIN_EMAIL` and `AUDIT_ADMIN_PASSWORD` are both unset. Creating/changing a production identity requires owner action; the owner session will not be used without specific approval. |
| #77 server-enforced QA prefix | **OPEN:** no cheap existing server-enforced mechanism was established by reading the issue. This run uses `qa-audit-2026-10-28-` for any authorized QA row, records each before deletion, and starts with zero new QA rows. |
| #78 pre-publish schema check | **PROCESS READY:** the loaded `replit-deploy` runbook diffs `shared/schema.ts` and `migrations/` before publish. Any such diff or unrun production migration requires explicit owner confirmation before publish. |
| #84 merge-gate script | **OPEN / NOT IMPLEMENTED HERE:** current project policy prohibits new validation scripts/gates. Only type-check/build are allowed before an owner-reviewed PR; the owner merges. |
| #85 stale skill edits | **MITIGATED FOR THIS RUN:** all source inspection and edits use a fresh worktree from current `origin/main`, not stale/untracked skill copies. |
| PR #27 Cloudflare | **OPEN, UNMERGED, OUT OF PRODUCTION-AUDIT SCOPE:** no merge, DNS, route, identity, scheduler or deployment change will be made. |

## Researcher-specific unresolved history

- FIX-P / PR #129 offline first-load Resources state has no prior production proof and remains UNVERIFIED.
- PR #145 replaced empty built-in WebSearch with Bing RSS; #146 then replaced that undeployed approach with GitHub REST Search. Do not report Bing as the final production behavior.
- PR #147 fixed a real production `Promise.withResolvers` rejection. Verification must use the deployed runtime/log/audit trail, not type-check alone.
- PR #148 moved `research_finished` to a shared success/failure path. A valid production check requires an existing post-deploy terminal run and its audit row; no paid run is triggered by this audit.

## Appendix B — exhaustive structured finding-ID cross-check

Added (UTC): 2026-10-29T01:31:43.161537+00:00

The full 931-file corpus produced 3,222 candidate records and 1,257 structurally valid unique finding IDs after excluding non-numeric file/path tokens. Each row cites the first and latest candidate record. This is an exhaustive recall-oriented index, **not** a list of confirmed defects: some IDs are interaction rows, duplicates across resumed sessions, or later-dismissed candidates. The raw records are in `.local/session-mining/all-corpus-bug-candidates.json`; statuses must be read from the excerpts and current production re-checks. No credential values are included.

## Editorial note

The phrase “Appendix B” in the PR re-check contract refers to the cycle-7 defect/fix history above and, for older records, Appendix A. Appendix B as subsequently added is the exhaustive structured-ID cross-check.

## Phase 1 — deployed route inventory

- Crawled 62 destinations in isolated Chrome: core public routes, nested taxonomy and aliases, auth/guard redirects, member surfaces, admin guards, not-found paths, and crawl-control documents. Evidence batches: `e2e-evidence/run-20261029T010233Z-full-functional/step-04-route-crawl-batch1.json`, `step-05-route-crawl-batch2.json`, `step-06-route-crawl-batch3.json`, `step-07-route-crawl-aliases-meta.json`, `step-08-iostvos-aliases.json`.
- Confirmed `/sub-subcategory/iostvos` and `/subsubcategory/iostvos` redirect to canonical `/sub-subcategory/ios-tvos`; `/settings/notifications` and bare `/iostvos` are real 404s; `/sign-in/verify-email-address` returns to the normal sign-in card; `/robots.txt`, `/llms.txt`, and `/sitemap.xml` return 200.
- Current client source confirms 15 canonical admin strip tabs plus Sub-Subcategories, Digests and Journeys (18 admin sections total).
